2576
This paper introduces Tensor Product Generation Network (TPGN), combining deep learning with tensor product representations to integrate explicit language structures into NLP tasks like image captioning, enabling unsupervised learning of grammatical roles.
-----
-----
2732
This paper introduces a hierarchical predictor-corrector scheme using neural networks to control complex physical systems described by PDEs over long time frames by splitting the task into planning and control, trained with a differentiable PDE solver.
-----
-----
2736
This paper introduces SuperCaptioning, a method that combines language and vision processing in a single CNN model using two-dimensional word embeddings, achieving high-quality image captions as shown on the Flickr30k dataset.
-----
-----
2745
A method is proposed to incrementally learn an embedding space for network architectures, enabling efficient selection during compressed architecture search using Bayesian Optimization, outperforming baselines and surpassing ShuffleNet.
-----
-----
2814
This paper introduces two methods to analyze how input units interact to influence neural network outputs, focusing on local contextual effects. The approach was successfully applied to explain AlphaGo Zero's gaming strategies.
-----
-----
2894
This paper introduces a Bayesian model for inferring contrastive temporal logic specifications that highlight differences between two sets of plan traces, demonstrating its effectiveness across multiple planning domains and a simulated air combat scenario.
